### y2v6 p.250–257 — Ch.5: Nagumo's fifth win; Kiryūin's jab; the “price” warning (Horikita's POV)
[scene] (p.250) She's killing time in the cheering seats; Yagami from 1-B approaches — he compliments her class; Nagumo just ran through the ribbon — his fifth consecutive win; he looks expressionless; girls swarm him; he tells them he wants to be alone; he distances himself.
[says:yagami-takuya] (p.250) “Good work out there, Horikita-senpai.”
[says] (p.250) “Your class seems to be doing well, too. You’re a close second place.”
[says:yagami-takuya] (p.250) “You too, senpai. Aren’t you guys in first place? It’s difficult to imagine this is the same class that started as Class D last year!”
[says] (p.250) “Was that a compliment? Or was some sarcasm thrown in there too?”
[says:yagami-takuya] (p.250) “Not at all! I genuinely respect you, although not as much as Student Council President Nagumo.”
[says] (p.251) “He would usually at least pay lip service to them, but he doesn’t look the least bit happy,” I said.
[says:yagami-takuya] (p.251) “He is almost guaranteed to graduate in Class A, regardless of whether he wins or loses. So, maybe he can’t get fired up for this?”
[says] (p.251) “I’ll go have a chat with the Student Council President,” I said.
[says:yagami-takuya] (p.251) “Is that so? Well, I have my next competition, so I’ll excuse myself.”
[scene] (p.251–255) Kiryūin-senpai (3-B) confronts Nagumo near the finish area — “Congrats on the 5 wins in a row, Nagumo!” — “Why are you here?” — “…Seems like there are more than one or two people cheering for you.” — “Word around the block is that Ayanokouji is absent today. Is that why you look so upset?” — Nagumo sighs — “I thought at least he would finally quench my thirst, but it seems I had the wrong idea.” — Kiryūin needles him — “Poor little thing, how about I take you on?” — “What a pointless lie. Even if I wanted to, I can’t even imagine you seriously offering me a fight. Am I wrong?” — “Fufufu. You got me, huh?” — she advises him to retire gracefully — “You’ve already dominated our grade and are fixed at Class A. On top of that, you’ve achieved a lot as the student council president. That should be enough. I recommend you graduate quietly.” — “You’re giving me advice? What’s this change of heart?” — “Maybe you’re right.” — “Don’t worry, Kiryuuin. You don’t need to tell me; I’m not going to play around with Ayanokouji anymore. He chose not to fight me, and there’s no point in thinking about it too deeply.”
[says:kiryuin-fuuka] (p.251) “Congrats on the 5 wins in a row, Nagumo!”
[says:nagumo-miyabi] (p.251) “Why are you here?”
[says:kiryuin-fuuka] (p.251) “You don’t have to be so cold-hearted, you know? I’m just concerned that you don’t look all that happy despite winning. Seems like there are more than one or two people cheering for you.”
[says:nagumo-miyabi] (p.251) “Don’t make me laugh. As if winning a competition of that level would mean anything to me.”
[says:kiryuin-fuuka] (p.252) “Knowing you, you could’ve just gathered all the weaklings and have them compete against you for an easy first place. Is that why you look so upset?”
[says:nagumo-miyabi] (p.252) “I thought at least he would finally quench my thirst, but it seems I had the wrong idea.”
[says:kiryuin-fuuka] (p.252) “Poor little thing, how about I take you on?”
[says:nagumo-miyabi] (p.252) “What a pointless lie. Even if I wanted to, I can’t even imagine you seriously offering me a fight. Am I wrong?”
[says:kiryuin-fuuka] (p.252) “Fufufu. You got me, huh?” Kiryuuin-senpai confessed, shrugging as she drew closer to President Nagumo’s side. “I’ll be done with my minimum requirements after the next event. I’m planning to relax and watch the matches after that.”
[says:nagumo-miyabi] (p.252) “Of course you are.”

[says:kiryuin-fuuka] (p.253) “Maybe you’re right.”
[says:nagumo-miyabi] (p.253) “Don’t worry, Kiryuuin. You don’t need to tell me; I’m not going to play around with Ayanokouji anymore. He chose not to fight me, and there’s no point in thinking about it too deeply.”
[scene] (p.255) Kiryūin walks off; Nagumo turns to Horikita — “I kept you waiting, Suzune. Did you need anything from me?” — she covers for AK's absence; he reads her like a book — “Come with me for a bit during lunch break. We’ll meet at––” — she cannot refuse.
[says:kiryuin-fuuka] (p.255) “If he lost a direct confrontation against the Student Council President, he wouldn’t be able to move on as if nothing had happened. Try to understand why he ran away. He has such a cute side to him, doesn’t he?”

[says:nagumo-miyabi] (p.256) “I kept you waiting.”

[says:nagumo-miyabi] (p.257) “This might cause some trouble for your grade.”

### y2v6 p.243–249 — Ch.5: The 100m showdown; the long jump 2cm (Horikita's POV)

[says] (p.323) “That’s good enough for me.”

[says] (p.324) “I have learned a very important lesson from the Unanimous Consent test.”

[says] (p.324) “I did not make a mistake. I was able to recognize once again that I chose to keep Kushida-san, and that that choice was correct.”
[says] (p.324) “I know. There are still reasons for us to be worried. Especially when it comes to Hasebe-san; honestly, I still don’t know what I should do about her. But, if we face a similar test once again, I think I will be able to handle it much better next time.”
[says:ayanokouji-kiyotaka] (p.324) “And why do you think so?”

And I made that decision before I was even clear on whether I wanted to expel her. That was a mistake.”

[says] (p.325) “We got class points. However, we had to pay dearly for it. That special test has taught me a lot. It showed me both victory and defeat.”
[says:ayanokouji-kiyotaka] (p.325) “It’s not certain that you haven’t failed, however.”
[says] (p.325) “I’m just a second year high school student. I’m a child. Isn’t it okay for me to mess up?”

### y2v6 p.171–193 — Ch.3: Monday return; the walk to the café (AK POV)
[scene] (p.171) Monday's homeroom — she enters alone three minutes before the bell with a stiff expression, then walks the class's cold silence: Kushida goes first, Shinohara counters, and by afternoon she is walking beside AK towards the cafe in Keyaki Mall with her face showing him her strain.
[action] (p.171) She enters the class alone, three minutes before homeroom, with a stiff expression on her face, glances once at Kushida's seat, takes her own seat and stares directly at the board.
[says] (p.178) “I checked if she'd be fine with just me, but she said you were absolutely essential and didn't listen to me at all.”
[says] (p.178) “I treated her like a kid unbecoming of her age. To the point where I don't want to talk about it.”
[says] (p.179) “Anyway, it took a week but we've managed to gather everyone in the class.”
[says] (p.180) “Oh yeah. I want you to send a message to Student Council President Nagumo.”

[says] (p.181) “While I was waiting to meet with Kushida-san, I also checked on Miyake-kun and Yukimura-kun many times.”
[says] (p.184) “In a way, the problem has already been solved. You'd been absent from school for several days.”
[says] (p.185) “Could you stop beating around the bush and say what you want to say?”
[says] (p.185) “I'm not satisfied. There's still a gap of more than 500 points between us and Class A.”
[says] (p.186) “Indeed.”
[says] (p.187) “It's because I deemed it to be the right choice that I had the resolve to keep her in the class, taking on the animosity from everyone else.”
[says] (p.189) “… I can't fulfill a request that can't be done.”
[says] (p.190) “However, even if I were to go to the past with my memories as I am now, what I would most likely do wouldn't change much.”
[says] (p.192) “It's the same. You're just mad that one of your friends was sacrificed.”

[says] (p.193) “Everyone's like that. It isn't strange to want to save yourself.”

### y2v12 p.18 — Ch.1: "A battle through conversation"
[scene] (p.18) After Chabashira says even the teachers aren't told the details, Horikita stands and offers the first guess in the room: conversation as the exam's currency.
[says] (p.18) "...A battle through conversation... Could that be a possibility?"
[note] (p.16–17) She is named as the class leader on the rankings page ("Class B by Horikita"); her class has 38 students — exactly 3 representatives + 35 participants, so one absence costs 5 points.
[rel:ayanokouji-kiyotaka] (p.16–19) Notices his calm here the same as everyone else; his behavior matters more than she says aloud.

### y2v12 p.24–25 — Ch.1: Agrees to meet first
[scene] (p.24) That evening he messages her before the others; she had already intended to discuss the end-of-year special exam with him; the text he sends is worded to allow Friday–Sunday rather than only the weekend.
[scene] (p.25) About an hour after the first exchange she replies, asking to discuss the exam; place and time flexible; he decides to meet her at the Keyaki Mall café after school on Friday; she will be the first of the four meetings.
[rel:ayanokouji-kiyotaka] (p.24–25) Reaches out at the same time as everyone else; the Friday slot reserved for her.

<!-- walk: y2v12 ch1 → p.25 -->
### y2v12 p.26–37 — Ch.2: The Keyaki Mall café
[scene] (p.26) Friday after school, Keyaki Mall; she arrives first and waits; she bought tea instead of her usual coffee (needed sugar); she is tense; he ribs her about fewer first years around; they compare notes on the first years facing their own year-end exams.
[says] (p.26) "Is something wrong?"
[says] (p.26) "...Is it that obvious?"
[scene] (p.27–28) She shows him the first-year rankings on her phone (Class A 991, Class B 697, Class C 532, Class D 510); rationalizes away their comparable start; he notes the first years are coping surprisingly well; she half-retorts with Sudō (dead weight from enrollment).
[scene] (p.29–32) She asks for strategies; admits even Chabashira doesn't know the exam content; admits relief he has no secret way to win; floats Kōenji as their only hope; he channels his plan through Hirata and Kushida; says Sudō or Onodera wouldn't work (too specialized); then reveals he'd participate as the general — forcing her to weigh giving up the seat.
[says] (p.32) "Is it that obvious?"
[says:ayanokouji-kiyotaka] (p.32) "I said I wouldn't mind participating if you want."
[scene] (p.33–35) He sets terms: she fights as middle guard; he as general; his participation demands Kōenji-style detachment be acknowledged; she deliberates at length over pride and points — then accepts his premise.
[says] (p.33) "What if I said I can't give up being the general?"
[says:ayanokouji-kiyotaka] (p.33) "I'd just refuse."
[scene] (p.35–37) His fallback: if he's dragged out and wins, she keeps his cooperation; if she loses outright, he cooperates for six months; she checks for loopholes (vanguard sacrificing on purpose); then she accepts formally: he is the general, she is middle guard, she fights without a seat-change, and she must get the class's consent — with Kōenji's potential objection handled by Ayanokōji if needed; he also demands the billet be kept secret from Ichinose's class.
[says] (p.36) "If you’re willing to serve as the general… everything else is trivial."
[rel:ayanokouji-kiyotaka] (p.26–37) Accepts him as general; concedes the seat; agrees to keep his role hidden; delegates class consent handling.

<!-- walk: y2v12 ch2 → p.62 -->

[scene] (p.64) Exam morning; she waits at the door with Ayanokōji and Hirata, restless and short-tempered; she complains when they arrive "the last ones"; she exhales when the entrance is granted; the tension grows as names are called.
[says] (p.64) "You’re quite late. We’re the last ones."

[says] (p.67) "What are you doing...!"
[scene] (p.69–83) The monitor shows her battle order — middle guard vs Ichinose — wait, vs Kanzaki (Class D middle guard); she confirms the opponent tree with him; she asks his read; they size up Ichinose, Ryūen, Sakayanagi; he maneuvers her into asking for the traitor right — she balks, then takes his collateral (full cooperation) with a grin.
[says] (p.82) "Let me talk about something unrelated to forming groups first. It’s about the right to designate a traitor. Could you let me have it?"
[rel:ayanokouji-kiyotaka] (p.64–83) Poke-induced calm; trades groups for traitor rights; the general question hangs over her like weather.
[rel:hirata-yousuke] (p.64–83) Waits with him; builds groups together; the two-front worry.

[says] (p.100) “Thank you. You should rest for now.”

### y2v12 p.119–128 — Ch.6: The middle-guard battle
[scene] (p.119) In the waiting room, the countdown begins; she waits for the middle-guard match; she imagines Kanzaki's game-plan — defensive, like Hirata or Hamaguchi — and wonders if offense is likelier.
[says] (p.119) “But, there’s also a good chance he might go on the offensive to change the flow...”
[scene] (p.120) Kanzaki enters with four minutes left; he surveys the room; sits; refuses small talk — asks her bluntly whose idea it was to make Ayanokōji the general, how and when it was decided.
[says] (p.120) “Whose idea was it to put Ayanokōji as the general?”
[says] (p.120) “That’s quite a sudden question.”
[says] (p.120) “Whoever decided to make him the general, for what reason, and when, is our choice to make, isn’t it?”
[says] (p.120) “Maybe. Perhaps even he’s changing little by little.”
[says] (p.120) “Are we done here? I’d like to focus on the exam.”
[scene] (p.120–122) The match begins; she selects the bold/poker-face group (Ijūin, Sudō, Miyake, Ichihashi, Onodera, Nishimura, Matsushita); in round three, Miyake claims graduate; she reads him as honor student — both nominate; she is right; Kanzaki loses four lives (7→3).
[scene] (p.122) She considers a second discussion group; Ichinose's side faces the same problem; then she pauses — Kanzaki approaches her after the first discussion ends.
[scene] (p.122–126) He pleads for concession — to be let into the end-of-year win via her mercy; he apologizes, bows so low his head touches his knees; she refuses flatly; he claims that Ichinose must feel as he does — there is no chance of winning once Ayanokōji walked in; Horikita bristles — don't underestimate Ichinose.
[says] (p.122) “Are you serious? Sorry, but those words are difficult to believe.”
[says] (p.123) “If you understand, then you shouldn’t be making such an impossible request in the first place.”
[says] (p.124) “You’re underestimating Ichinose-san, who’s the leader of your class.”
[says] (p.124) “I don’t like it.”
[says] (p.126) “I won’t hold back.”
[scene] (p.126–128) She tells him at least not to lie to himself; he can't win; she fights at full strength; in the second discussion he passes blankly, waits to lose; she nominates — Mine-kun is an honor student; Kanzaki hits zero; the battle is hers, 7-0.
[says] (p.127) “Have you given up?”
[says] (p.127) “...Then you should face this special exam with the resolve to defeat me and Ayanokōji-kun alone.”
[thinks] (p.128) “I’m fighting to move up to Class A. That’s my goal...”
[rel:kanzaki-ryuuji] (p.119–128) He comes to her begging; she wins as cleanly as an exam can be won; she refuses to gloat but does not flinch from the win.
[rel:sudou-ken] (p.121) She watches him composed on the monitor and nearly cries with pride.

### y2v12 p.132–141 — Ch.6: The generals' battle
[scene] (p.132) Ichinose appears about five minutes later; Horikita observes every move; Ichinose notes Horikita's change — presence softened, easier to approach; Horikita denies, then concedes she might be.
[says] (p.132) “...It’s just a coincidence.”
[says] (p.132) “Is that so? I don’t think I’ve changed at all...”
[says] (p.132) “...Really? It’s not my intention to be like that.”

[scene] (p.137) Round two — Ichinose nominates ANOTHER executive role (Minami) — Horikita burns a nomination (incorrect) — she loses one life for getting it wrong AND one for Ichinose's hit; dizzied, she nearly loses her nerve.
[says] (p.137) “Did you really know that Minami-san had an executive role?”
[scene] (p.138) Round three — Ichinose passes; Horikita identifies an executive role (Hatsutori) — an underclassman — she recovers only one life; two honor students still unaccounted for.
[scene] (p.139) She probes whether Ichinose heard Kanzaki's despair before the exam — “You’re being mischievous, aren’t you?” — Ichinose denies point-blank and explains she deduced it herself.
[says] (p.139) “You’re being mischievous, aren’t you?”
[says] (p.139) “You heard from Kanzaki-kun, didn’t you?”
[scene] (p.140) Ichinose claims to know all the honor students — “I’m fine” — Horikita can feel her defeat being decided in real time; Ichinose's eyes meet hers and she softly smiles and doesn't look away.
[thinks] (p.140) “‘I’m fine’? What does that even mean?”
[says] (p.140) “...What are you... intending...”
[scene] (p.141) Horikita slaps her own cheeks — defeat not yet certain; she can still drag it to a draw; she reopens her eyes on the monitor — the opponent isn't a machine; Ichinose must make mistakes.
[note] (p.141–146) The rest of the battle is not printed in this volume's text; the waiting-room announcement (p.146) shows her remaining lives: 0 — she is defeated by Ichinose.
[rel:ichinose-honami] (p.132–141) The generals' battle — Ichinose reads Horikita completely; Horikita loses without landing a decisive hit.
[rel:ayanokouji-kiyotaka] (p.141–151) Her hope is him; he finds her on the hallway after her loss and embraces her — "You have allies."

<!-- walk: y2v12 ch6 → p.152 -->

### y2v12 p.220–221 — Short Story: Familiar Everyday Life
[scene] (p.220) She sits at the café where he'll meet her; the exam is announced; she muses on never having imagined this life; she notes the passing of time.
[thinks] (p.221) “Two years ago, following my brother, I enrolled into this school as well.”
[thinks] (p.221) “Well... I wonder whether counting those two as friends is appropriate.”
[scene] (p.221) She feels embarrassed yet proud of her changing self — the distance between enrollment-day Horikita and now; he arrives; the day is ordinary, and that is the treasure.
[rel:ayanokouji-kiyotaka] (p.220–221) The person at the center of all her growth; she waits for him in the café of an exam-day Thursday.
[rel:kushida-kikyou] (p.221) One of the two she wonders about — she counts them as friends, cautiously.
[rel:ibuki-mio] (p.221) One of the two she wonders about — she counts them as friends, cautiously.

### y2v12.5 p.11–22 — Ch.1: The class debrief after the special exam
[scene] (p.11) The representatives return to the classroom; Horikita will explain what she can share about the exam.
[says] (p.13) “I’m sorry, but could everyone please stay for a while?”
[says] (p.13) “Yes. I judged that we can’t leave any unresolved issues from this end-of-year special exam. But I can’t do it alone. Could I get everyone’s cooperation?”
[says] (p.13) “Including myself, I want to clear up what seems to be our biggest concern first.”
[says] (p.14) “In my opinion... There was definitely a risk of expulsion for the student assigned the role of traitor, but if the rules were properly understood, the risk of expulsion was almost zero. I thought Maezono-san and Ayanokōji-kun understood that well...”
[says] (p.14) “...Yes, she was strong. I couldn’t envision any way to win.”
[says] (p.15) “Expelling Maezono... the traitor, how does that lead to victory?”
[says] (p.15) “Mutually using the rights of the traitor... Indeed, it would be easy to establish with Ichinose-san, and it indirectly implicates her in Maezono-san’s expulsion... That was your aim...”
[says] (p.17) “...There’s a convincing reason behind it—is what you’re trying to say?”
[says] (p.20) “I really couldn’t stand against Ichinose-san.”
[says] (p.20) “It was... necessary for our class to win.”
[rel:ayanokouji-kiyotaka] (p.11–22) She reports her defeat to Ichinose honestly and, at the end, backs Ayanokōji's decision as class leader.
[rel:hirata-yousuke] (p.15–22) He objects; she does not call a vote.
[rel:ichinose-honami] (p.14–20) She lost to Ichinose in the representative battle.

### y3v1 p.10–12 — Prologue: the third-year classroom and the Class 3-A nameplate
[scene] (p.10) On the first day of Class 3-A, Horikita climbs to the third-year floor before classes and stops before the newly polished "Class 3-A" nameplate.
[narrates] (p.10) "I've finally made it here…"
[narrates] (p.10) "From Class 1-D to Class 3-A."
[narrates] (p.10) She entered school only to follow after her older brother, who kept his distance at first; school life taught her that chasing him was not enough, and she came to understand his true feelings.
[narrates] (p.10) She is on the student council and read a congratulatory speech at the entrance ceremony; she must not be complacent.
[narrates] (p.11) "If he hadn't been in the same class, I certainly wouldn't be who I am now."
[narrates] (p.11) She set her sights on graduating from Class A not just for her brother or herself, but to share the joy with everyone in the class, starting with Ayanokōji.
[narrates] (p.11) Ryūen's class is catching up fast; Ichinose's class and the former Sakayanagi class must not be underestimated either.
[narrates] (p.12) "To think I'd be next to him again, huh…?"
[trivia] (p.10–12) Her seat is the second row from the corridor, fourth from the front; next to it (first row, fourth seat) is Ayanokōji-kun's name — the same desk pairing as their first year.
[trait] (p.10–12) Grateful, determined and privately anxious — resolves to graduate from Class A with this class and these companions, and not to let it end as just a dream.
[rel:ayanokouji-kiyotaka] (p.11–12) Attributes who she has become to his presence and is quietly delighted to be seated beside him again; the seating cheer is cut short by his unexplained absence later that morning.
[rel:horikita-manabu] (p.10) Her older brother (unnamed in the text; the roster's horikita-manabu), whom she followed into the school and whose distancing she first resented and later came to understand.
[rel:ryuuen-kakeru] (p.11) Notes his class is catching up quickly behind Class A.
[rel:ichinose-honami] (p.11) Acknowledges her class, like the former Sakayanagi class, must not be underestimated.
[rel:sakayanagi-arisu] (p.11) Mentions the former Sakayanagi class as a rival to watch.

### y3v1 p.13–20 — The transfer announcement shocks Class A
[scene] (p.13) Notices Ayanokōji absent on the first day of Class 3-A; discusses with Sudō whether he's feigning sickness over the Karuizawa breakup.
[says] (p.15) "Once is okay. But if it happens repeatedly, as a class, we can't just ignore it."
[says] (p.16) "Sensei, are you alright?"
[says] (p.20) "What are you talking about…? Something like Ayanokouji-kun transferring to Class C…"
[trait] (p.13–20) Initially calm and analytical, but the transfer announcement shatters her composure — her voice trembles, her heart races, and she can't accept what the OAA confirms.
[rel:ayanokouji-kiyotaka] (p.13–20) Deeply shaken by his unannounced transfer; refuses to believe it at first.

### y3v1 p.24–28 — The class debates; Kushida and Hirata's composure
[says] (p.26) "Yes… Setting aside Ayanokouji-kun's intentions, there's no denying he's the ideal recruit for Class C's comeback."
[says] (p.27) "But that's… that's not proof. He might just be sick, or it could be some sort of a mistake…"
[trait] (p.24–28) Clings to the hope of a mistake; notes Chabashira's shock proves Ayanokōji was too important to lose; rattled by Kushida's pointed questions and Hirata's calm acceptance.
[rel:kushida-kikyou] (p.25–28) Kushida passes her the question of Ayanokōji's importance; later needles Hirata's uncharacteristic calm.
[rel:hirata-yousuke] (p.27–28) He urges accepting reality; she can't match his composure.

### y3v1 p.29–33 — Rushing to confront Ayanokōji; self-doubt
[scene] (p.29) After school, resolves to confirm Ayanokōji's true intentions, trembling at the thought of seeing him.
[narrates] (p.29) "I wanted to see him."
[narrates] (p.29) "I didn't want to see him."
[narrates] (p.29) "Honestly, I did feel a bit scared about seeing him."
[narrates] (p.29) "That was a lie. I was very scared."
[narrates] (p.33) "Ayanokouji-kun… Why…"
[narrates] (p.33) "…Am I really that unreliable?"
[trait] (p.29–33) Tortured by indecision and fear, then bitter self-doubt — wonders if she seemed like a child to him, unworthy of being relied upon; resolves to save him if the transfer wasn't his will.
[rel:hirata-yousuke] (p.30) He tries to speak; she brushes him off to go meet Ayanokōji.
[rel:ayanokouji-kiyotaka] (p.29–33) Desperate to hear his intentions from his own lips before deciding anything.

### y3v1 p.34–38 — The café confrontation; Ayanokōji's cold exposure
[scene] (p.34) Arrives at the Keyaki Mall café with Matsushita; finds Ayanokōji with Hashimoto, Morishita and Ichinose, conversing indifferently.
[says] (p.35) "I want you to let me have a conversation with Ayanokouji-kun."
[says] (p.35) "…Leader candidate… That's quite a sudden thing to say."
[says] (p.35) "Sorry, but I need you to be quiet right now, Hashimoto-kun. As the class leader, I have to grasp the current situation."
[says] (p.36) "I see. Indeed, it must look like a strange transfer to everyone but a few students."
[trait] (p.34–38) Tries to stay calm and assert her role as leader, but is repeatedly blocked by Hashimoto and stunned as Ayanokōji coldly exposes Matsushita's behind-the-scenes cooperation — realizing the ground has shifted to the opponent's territory.
[rel:ayanokouji-kiyotaka] (p.35–38) Confronts him; he refuses to explain, reveals he told no one in Class A, and casually exposes his secret partnership with Matsushita — making clear he is no longer an ally.
[rel:hashimoto-masayoshi] (p.35) Blocks her from reaching Ayanokōji as his "leader candidate" gatekeeper.
[rel:matsushita-chiaki] (p.36) Accompanies her; Ayanokōji reveals Matsushita was one of his behind-the-scenes collaborators (even with Maezono's expulsion), shaking Horikita.
[rel:ichinose-honami] (p.37) Watches keenly; says she has much to learn about the students and will pay close attention to Matsushita.

### y3v1 p.98–107 — A week on: grief, the exam announcement, and self-loathing
[scene] (p.98) Sits alone before homeroom, a week after the transfer; observes the class adapting as if nothing happened.
[narrates] (p.98) "They were trying to alter their two years with Ayanokouji-kun as if it had never happened."
[narrates] (p.99) "Was this class not good enough?"
[narrates] (p.99) "If I had been more reliable, could he have stayed without transferring?"
[narrates] (p.100) "Did you hear me? Horikita."
[narrates] (p.100) "Sorry, yes. It's okay, I'm listening." — a lie; she heard nothing
[trait] (p.98–107) Heartbroken and unable to focus — Matsushita smiles less, Sudō gets irritable, and she herself is "desperately trying to maintain Class A's image by acting calmly"; realizes with self-loathing that she feels relief at not facing Ayanokōji (Class D is their opponent).
[rel:ayanokouji-kiyotaka] (p.98–107) Her grief over his absence grows daily; regrets the feelings at the celebration party that never reached him.
[rel:matsushita-chiaki] (p.98) Notices she smiles and talks less.
[rel:sudou-ken] (p.98, 106) Notices he gets irritated over trivial matters again; he checks on her when she zones out.

### y3v1 p.126–135 — The bench with Kushida; the collapse in her room
[scene] (p.126) Sits gloomily on a bench on the way back to the dorm; Kushida stops and needles her.
[narrates] (p.127) "Why the questioning tone?"
[narrates] (p.127) "You're just incredibly clingy, aren't you?"
[narrates] (p.131) "I'm not in the mood to do anything right now."
[narrates] (p.132) "Ambushing him in a place like this—doing such a thing… there's no way he'd be happy about it…"
[narrates] (p.134) "…Again, he made me cry…"
[narrates] (p.134) "Why…?"
[trait] (p.126–135) Devastated and stuck — Kushida's cold words make her realize she was "playing the part of a pitiful Horikita"; goes home, collapses on her bed in uniform, cries again, and dwells on wishing she could turn back time to grab Ayanokōji's arm.
[rel:kushida-kikyou] (p.126–132) Kushida sees through her, calls her clingy, criticizes her gloomy face as bad for morale, and leaves cold words ("I doubt he'll care").
[rel:ibuki-mio] (p.131) Kushida says Ibuki has been coming to her for food since Horikita stopped cooking.
[rel:hirata-yousuke] (p.133) Hirata messages her to discuss the exam, pulling her slightly back to reality.

### y3v1 p.180–183 — Class A's loss; Horikita's regret
[says] (p.183) "I'm sorry, everyone… It's because I… couldn't do anything…"
[trivia] (p.180–185) Class A loses to Class D; Kōenji (who had a positive attitude toward written exams) is targeted with 25 penalty points — Hirata suspects leaked information while Karuizawa blames Ayanokōji.
[trait] (p.180–183) Racked with guilt and self-loathing over the loss, apologizes to the class; Hirata and Karuizawa try to keep morale up by noting they haven't lost yet.
[rel:hirata-yousuke] (p.183) He calmly urges the class to keep fighting and not lose motivation.
[rel:karuizawa-kei] (p.183) She suspects Ayanokōji leaked information about Kōenji.

### y3v1 p.203–209 — Alone in the dark: guilt, then Ibuki's kick
[scene] (p.203) Stays alone in Class A's empty classroom after the loss, blaming herself for failing to devise even one strategy in the wake of Ayanokōji's transfer.
[narrates] (p.203) "I should go home…"
[trivia] (p.203) No one blamed her — they comforted her saying there'd be a next time, but their warm words hardly registered; she sat mind-void until she was the last one left, the classroom dyed in twilight.
[trivia] (p.204–207) Walks the deserted corridor in a loop of self-doubt ("I'm becoming useless"), then outside onto gravel — and is suddenly kicked in the back without warning, thrown forward, hands and knees stinging, bag rolling away in dust.
[narrates] (p.207) "What… What was that!?"
[narrates] (p.207) "What are you doing…? You're out of your mind."
[narrates] (p.208) "I don't understand what you mean."
[trait] (p.203–208) Paralyzed by guilt and loneliness, unable to recover; even when kicked she can't retaliate, noting small amounts of blood seeping into her palm.
[rel:ibuki-mio] (p.207–208) Kicks her from behind with full force, calls her pathetic, and says her spineless appearance irritates her; leaves after venting.

### y3v1 p.209–215 — The bench with Karuizawa: a heart-to-heart
[scene] (p.209) Crouches on the ground in pain when Karuizawa, still in uniform, comes over from a nearby bench, concerned, and offers to call a teacher.
[narrates] (p.209) "I'm fine… the pain is finally starting to subside. She really has no common sense…"
[narrates] (p.210) "I'm really hopeless right now…"
[narrates] (p.210) "I'm sorry about today's exam as well. I couldn't lead the class to victory."
[narrates] (p.210) "It's not true. I thought I was quite strong myself, but it wasn't me who was strong…"
[narrates] (p.210) "I realized that it was only because Ayanokouji-kun was in the class that I was able to stand up straight."
[narrates] (p.211) "What kind of person was Ayanokouji-kun to you…?"
[narrates:karuizawa-kei] (p.212) "Do you… like Ayanokouji-kun too?"
[narrates] (p.213) "I… haven't been taught anything by him…"
[narrates] (p.214) "I'm pathetic, aren't I…?"
[narrates] (p.215) "Now that it's come to this, I have no choice but to make him turn his head this way. And I will graduate with everyone in Class A. I promise."
[narrates] (p.215) "Not at all. I'm a weak person, but I've realized that I'm not alone."
[narrates] (p.215) "Yes—let's definitely make him regret it."
[trivia] (p.209–210) Karuizawa gently grabs her wrist to help her up (her palm bloodied and dirty) and brushes dirt off her uniform with a handkerchief; the hand that should have hurt forgot its pain.
[trivia] (p.211–213) Karuizawa reveals Ayanokōji was "someone I love," refuses to say whether the transfer was his idea ("Not saying it… that's become my reason for existence"), and tells her the secret of her first year — middle-school bullying, climbing the social hierarchy, fake romance with Hirata, the renewal of bullying and her rescue (itself orchestrated), and the rooftop battle with Ryūen.
[inference] (p.212–215) Karuizawa's question makes her recall spring break — a pleasant, embarrassing pounding in her chest she can't deny; she realizes she was left out, having known only Ayanokōji's back, and cries at the unfairness of it all.
[trait] (p.203–215) Breaks down, then slowly recovers — realizing she must make Ayanokōji "turn his head this way" and graduate from Class A with everyone; for the first time takes a step forward in reality and in her heart.
[rel:karuizawa-kei] (p.209–215) The two bond over being "involved with a really difficult person"; Karuizawa shares her painful past, and they resolve together to make Ayanokōji regret transferring out.
[rel:ibuki-mio] (p.211–212) Considers that Ibuki might have been worried in her own way; she had been lingering by the bench as if waiting for someone.
[rel:ayanokouji-kiyotaka] (p.210–215) Realizes he never turned around or waited for her, that she knew him less than anyone; resolves to make him look back.

### y3v2 p.96–114 — The wagyu dinner; probing Ibuki and Kushida about Ayanokōji
[scene] (p.96) Cooks a meal (kuroge wagyu hamburger steak, rice) for Ibuki and Kushida in her room, intending to ask their opinions.
[trivia] (p.96) The ingredients cost twice her usual budget; she notes the noisy voices of her two guests now reach her "like strangely calming music" — a shift in perspective.
[trivia] (p.96–97) Admits to herself she told Ibuki a small lie — that she has fully recovered from Ayanokōji's departure; her wounded heart is far from healed, and the lie was meant to prevent another victim of Ibuki's kicks and to deceive herself.
[says] (p.101) "I want to hear both of your opinions."
[says] (p.101) "Regarding Ayanokouji-kun, who has become quite the topic of conversation."
[says] (p.101) "It seems that when you think of 'defeating him,' you only consider it in a physical sense."
[says] (p.104) "Wanting to know his roots, I actually went to the three-way conference hoping to meet his parents."
[says] (p.104) "It was his father who came for the interview, and while he had a somewhat stern look, he was a calm, ordinary person."
[says] (p.104) "Even among them, Ayanokouji-kun feels... anomalous... as if he alone is clearly standing on a different plane."
[inference] (p.102–109) Reconstructs Ayanokōji's background: Ibuki and Kushida both suspect he has a "secret," Yagami and Amasawa knew him and bore resentment, and he avoided leaving any trace (no middle-school records online) — concluding their relationship was more than mere acquaintance.
[trait] (p.96–114) Vulnerable under the confident surface — still wounded by the transfer, deifying Ayanokōji to preserve her peace of mind, yet methodically gathering intelligence.
[rel:ibuki-mio] (p.96–110) Feeds her and probes her for insights into Ayanokōji; Ibuki says beating him is impossible and calls her "useless."
[rel:kushida-kikyou] (p.96–113) Shares the meal; Kushida reveals Yagami was not actually her kouhai and that Ayanokōji got him expelled to save her.
[rel:ayanokouji-atsuomi] (p.104) Met his father at the three-way conference — "calm, ordinary," claimed nothing special about his education; she suspects a lie.

### y3v2 p.111–114 — Kushida's goodbye; the conduct-exam breakthrough
[scene] (p.111) After Ibuki leaves, Kushida lingers and thanks her for the meal before departing.
[says] (p.112) "But Class C has Ayanokouji-kun."
[says] (p.112) "It's strange, isn't it? ... just hearing his name makes my body instinctively tense up and feel intimidated."
[says] (p.112) "Even so, I'll fight with the belief that I can win."
[says] (p.113) "I just love the cute me who pretends to be kind."
[says] (p.113) "I expect your strength isn't just for show."

[inference] (p.114) Noticing the mess Ibuki left (spilled miso soup, rice grains), and recalling Chabashira's coldness and "show me you've grown," she realizes the exam likely observes daily conduct/manners — and must secure Kōenji's cooperation by tomorrow.

### y3v2 p.115–124 — Enlisting Sudō and Onodera; the Kōenji deal
[scene] (p.115) Visits Sudō's room after 8 p.m. to explain her conduct-exam theory; Onodera is there studying with him.
[says] (p.115) "I apologize for visiting without notice. I have something I'd like to talk about."
[says] (p.117) "Your guess isn't technically wrong, Onodera-san."
[says] (p.117) "That's how I interpreted it."
[says] (p.118) "I want to share this with the entire class today and have them lead a thoroughly disciplined life starting tomorrow."
[says] (p.119) "But that's impossible, ain't it? Gettin' that guy to do what he's told." — Sudō
[says] (p.119) "Simply asking him will likely be impossible. However, I have an idea."
[says] (p.121) "If you exhibit no problematic behavior whatsoever, just like the other classmates, I will pay you 200,000 Private Points."
[says] (p.121) "Coincidentally, that is precisely what I was thinking, myself." — Kōenji
[says] (p.122) "The deal is done. And don't speak of this contract to anyone."
[inference] (p.114–124) Concludes from Chabashira's coldness and "show me you've grown" that the exam observes daily conduct; identifies Kōenji as the one obstacle who could drag Class A down and secures his "discipline" for 500,000 points.
[trait] (p.115–124) Decisive and calculating — shares the plan only with trusted students, takes a gamble on the conduct theory, and accepts Kōenji's extortion because ordinary requests wouldn't work with him.
[rel:sudou-ken] (p.115–119) Brings him along to Kōenji's room as backup; he readily agrees.
[rel:onodera-kayano] (p.115–119) Explains the plan to her too; she is studying with Sudō.
[rel:kouenji-rokusuke] (p.120–124) Negotiates in his room; he doubles her 200,000 offer to 500,000 and sees through her bluff about the upper limit.
[rel:karuizawa-kei] (p.118) Recalls her fear that the information might reach Class D.

[scene] (p.231) Meets Karuizawa near the dorm lobby on a Saturday before 11 a.m., in uniform for a 2 p.m. Student Council matter.

[rel:amasawa-ichika] (p.235–237) Tails her through Keyaki Mall, noting she acts alone and forms no close relationships; loses the lead when a message calls Horikita away.

### y3v2 p.237–247 — The unexplained message; Ishigami's consultation
[scene] (p.237) Receives an unregistered text, "I'm waiting in the Student Council room," and rushes to school, but the room is empty — a prank.
[says] (p.241) "Were you the one who sent me the message?"
[says] (p.241) "So you're not going to start with denial."
[says] (p.243) "To know your enemy, you must get inside their camp."
[says] (p.244) "If that's what you wish, I promise to protect your anonymity."
[says] (p.244) "Perhaps she simply wasn't telling the truth?"
[says] (p.245) "I can't doubt her without reason."
[says] (p.246) "Yes, thank you. Even if I'm pressed for information, I'll protect you at all costs."
[inference] (p.241–247) Ishigami reveals Nanase knew Ayanokōji from early on and may have orchestrated the first-year alliance just to contact him — a possibility Horikita can't dismiss, though Nanase had told her she didn't know.
[trait] (p.241–247) Sharp and methodical — deduces Ishigami sent the message and confirmed she was alone before approaching; promises anonymity and presses for the truth, feeling she is "sinking into a swamp."
[rel:ishigami-kyou] (p.238–247) He sends the message, reveals Nanase as the person who may know Ayanokōji's past, and agrees to probe on the condition he stay anonymous.
[rel:nanase-tsubasa] (p.244–246) Learned from Ishigami that she may know Ayanokōji's past; suspects she lied when she said she didn't.
[rel:ayanokouji-kiyotaka] (p.246–247) Pushes deeper into his past, unknowingly at the start of a "great change" — an orchestrated encounter.

### y2v2 p.23–38 — Horikita's cover story for Ayanokōji's perfect score
[scene] (p.23–27) She takes the floor and spins the "reserve strategy" — claiming she discovered his math talent last spring and asked him to hold back; the class is skeptical.
[says] (p.23) "Yukimura-kun and I- no, the entire 2nd year couldn't get this perfect score, so how was Ayanokouji-kun able to achieve that?"
[scene] (p.27–34) She argues that hiding his strength protected him from becoming a target, and that the class has grown enough to protect him now; Yousuke and Kei back her up.
[says] (p.27) "From the very start he had the knowledge to get perfect scores in math. As I knew this before anyone else, I thought up a little strategy."
[scene] (p.38) She closes the topic and leaves first, letting Ayanokōji handle the rest.
[trait] (p.23–38) Quick-witted and eloquent under pressure — she improvises a convincing cover story without prior preparation.
[rel:ayanokouji-kiyotaka] (p.23–38) She protects him by fabricating a strategic narrative.

[says] (p.317) "I see. It's true that I'm not as good as you at taking care of myself. I admit that."

[says] (p.318) "Me and Ibuki?"
[says] (p.318) "That's true, having some kind of insurance would be better… All right, I'll hear her out."
[trait] (p.316–318) Plans to go solo to act as the situation demands; defends her health (illness last island exam + winter fever) but concedes Ayanokōji's perfect attendance; refuses to fight him, and agrees to hear out grouping with Ibuki as insurance.
[rel:ayanokouji-kiyotaka] (p.316–318) Debates the solo-exam risks; he proposes she group with Ibuki.
[rel:ibuki-mio] (p.318) Agrees to hear out a partnership with her.
[rel:kouenji-rokusuke] (p.317) One of the three in Class D still ungrouped (with Horikita and Ayanokōji).

### y2v2 p.348–350 — Short story: "He Who Stands Beside Me" — joining the council
[scene] (p.348) From her own POV, recounts Nagumo's condition that she state why she wants to join the student council at this point in time.
[narrates] (p.348) "You wish to join the student council?" — Nagumo
[narrates] (p.348) "Tell me the reason why you, at this point in time, want to join the student council."
[narrates] (p.349) "I had a discord with my brother. And I enrolled into this school to make peace."
[narrates] (p.349) "I spent a whole year without being able to have a properly talk with with until the very last moments."
[narrates] (p.350) "Welcome to the Student Council. I'll have you work hard as a council member from today, Suzune." — Nagumo
[trait] (p.348–350) Honest and self-reflective — gives a truthful answer about her unresolved relationship with Manabu (not mentioning Ayanokōji), realizes she unconsciously values Ayanokōji highly and wants *him* to acknowledge her; resolves to grow through learning from Nagumo so they can reach Class A.
[rel:horikita-manabu] (p.348–349) Her brother; she joined to make peace but never could before he graduated.
[rel:ayanokouji-kiyotaka] (p.349–350) Wants his acknowledgment — a feeling she only admits to herself here.
[rel:nagumo-miyabi] (p.348–350) Sets the condition, welcomes her, calls her "Suzune."

### y2v3 p.44–50 — Horikita before and after disembarking
[says] (p.44) "Good morning. Did you sleep well yesterday?"
[says] (p.45) "I see you're still patronizing me about what happened last year."
[says] (p.45) "You're awfully chipper even though such a serious special exam is about to start."
[says] (p.45) "Yes, I have. I'm just glad that it didn't happen to one of our classmates."
[says] (p.46) "Mine is F9. Looks like the two of us have different Tables."
[says] (p.46) "That… would be troublesome."
[says] (p.47) "I lent you some points just the other day. I'd be mad if I didn't get any back."
[says] (p.47) "I know you have that contract with Kushida-san, so I can't blame you for being troubled for money."
[says] (p.48) "Thanks for your most exceptional advice then."
[says] (p.50) "What do you want?" — to Ibuki
[says] (p.50) "I don't see myself as her rival at all though?"
[trait] (p.44–50) Chipper and confident (leagues ahead of a year ago); her Table differs from Ayanokōji's; says she'd be "troubled" if he were expelled (jokingly citing the points she lent him); heads east; wears a hat (he can't afford one); Ibuki declares she'll never lose to her.
[rel:ayanokouji-kiyotaka] (p.44–48) Chats before departing; would be troubled by his expulsion; he warns her about going solo.
[rel:ibuki-mio] (p.50) She approaches to declare she'll never lose; Horikita doesn't see her as a rival.
[rel:kouenji-rokusuke] (p.48) Notes Kōenji strode past smiling — a coin flip whether he'll take the exam seriously.

### y2v3 p.363–364 — Short story: "A Brief Farewell" — leaving Ayanokōji at the starting line
[scene] (p.363) From her own POV, tells Ayanokōji she's leaving as she adjusts her sun hat, he staying at the starting line having seen her off.
[narrates] (p.363) "Well then, I'll be leaving."
[narrates] (p.363) "…no need to worry, huh?"
[narrates] (p.364) "As if… I guess that wasn't like me."
[narrates] (p.364) "First priority is to fight in the best way only I knew possible"
[trait] (p.363–364) Reflects she needn't worry about him — having watched him up close all year, always calm, always finding the right answer and winning; acknowledges his flaws (needing to reveal some ability, poor communication) but that he shields them using others; realizes she wants to become someone who can "cover his back, a partner he could rely on," but that mimicking his style is useless — her priority is to fight her own way on the island.
[rel:ayanokouji-kiyotaka] (p.363–364) She wants to become someone who can stand shoulder-to-shoulder with him and cover his back — a feeling she can't quite name.

### y2v3 p.43–46 — Horikita at the start of the island exam
[scene] (p.43–46) She walks with Ayanokōji at the start; they discuss their Tables, Kōenji's reliability, and point averaging.
[says] (p.46) "I lent you some points just the other day. I'd be mad if I didn't get any"
[trait] (p.43–46) Cautious but prepared — she has a contract with Kushida for points.
[rel:ayanokouji-kiyotaka] (p.43–46) They walk together.

### y2v4 p.19 — Horikita mentioned in passing

### y2v4 p.55–56 — Ch.2: Horikita rejoins him at F7
[scene] (p.55) She crosses paths with him at F7 — day eight, the first reunion since day one; she looks a little surprised but unusually fresh; she’s heading to G8, he’s to F8; they walk together.
[says] (p.55) “Ayanokōji-kun?”
[says:ayanokouji-kiyotaka] (p.55) “That it has.”
[says] (p.56) “I’m heading to G8. I ended up passing through here on the way. What about yourself?”
[says] (p.56) “Is it really so hard for you to believe that I’m still in such good shape?”
[says] (p.56) “Yes. To be exact, I heard about it today. In light of that, I’m glad that I ran into you.”
[says] (p.56) “Hmm. Did something unusual happen?”
[note] (p.56) Somebody from Class 2-A reached out to her near the starting area earlier today and urged her to meet with Sakayanagi; Sakayanagi told her of the retirements of Komiya and Kinoshita and proposed a plan; the two began to negotiate.
[trait] (p.55–56) Never in the bottom ten once during the exam — she moves with a strange calm; her interest in the cliff incident is sudden and purposeful; Sakayanagi’s contact reached her.
[rel:sakayanagi-arisu] (p.56) Summoned to meet her near the starting area; negotiated terms with her over the Shinohara rescue.

### y2v4 p.57 — Ch.2: The rescue, coordinated
[scene] (p.57) Walking with him, she relays the rescue plan and her own stake — a Task first place that rewarded three extra group slots; Shinohara seventh from the bottom and falling toward expulsion by day’s end.
[says] (p.57) “I had no reason to do that; I had to prevent Shinohara-san from getting kicked out of school. I heard that you were one of the first on the scene at the time, do you know the details of what happened?”
[says] (p.57) “Shinohara-san’s group ranking has been dropping lower and lower every day. Currently, she’s placed seventh from the bottom. At this rate, it seems she’ll fall far enough to qualify for expulsion by the end of the day today, so we have to hurry. If worse comes to worst and we can’t find another group for her to join, I’ll take matters into my own hands. Before I ran into you, I was lucky enough to secure first place in a Task that rewarded me with three additional spaces in my group.”
[says] (p.57) “At any rate, over these past eight days that I’ve been walking around the island, I’ve really come to realize that a lot more groups have walkie-talkies than I anticipated. I’ve seen students making use of them all over the place, Sakayanagi-san telling her fellow classmates about what happened to Shinohara-san is only one such example.”
[says] (p.58) “If we trusted one another a little more, I wonder… if we could’ve done that as well…?”
[says] (p.58) “That’s true enough.”
[trait] (p.57–58) Honest with him about her stake (Shinohara) and her prize (three extra slots); she notes the spread of walkie-talkies, especially among upper-year groups; she concedes that inter-class trust could have enabled the same among them.
[rel:shinohara-satsuki] (p.57) Seventh from bottom; fighting to prevent her expulsion today.
[rel:ayanokouji-kiyotaka] (p.55–59) Travels together; coordinates the rescue; both divert to the food Task.

### y2v4 p.58–59 — Ch.2: The food Task together
[scene] (p.58) Both nearly out of food; they change course to a participatory food Task with fifteen slots; the site swarms with first-, second- and third-year groups; they arrive too late for Early Bird Bonuses and spend the last minutes trading information.
[says] (p.58) “I don’t have that much food remaining either, so I’ll drop by the Task as well.”
[says] (p.59) “Look who’s talking. If you’ve really run low on food, then you ought to be a bit more pressed yourself.”
[says] (p.59) “And I’m in the same boat.”
[trait] (p.58–59) No Early Bird Bonuses today — with food at zero they chose the Task together; her stamina is as managed as his; she accepts the late arrival without complaint.

### y2v4 p.214–216 — Ch.6: The note; she marches north
[scene] (p.214) Day thirteen at dawn, she heads north toward I2 ignoring her designated area I7; the destination is written on a note found folded in her tent this morning — “Noon”, “K.A”, “Expelled”, “I2” — beautiful handwriting, foreign paper, the initials of Ayanokōji Kiyotaka.
[narrates] (p.215) “I2 referred to an area on the uninhabited island, and noon was the time.”
[narrates] (p.215) “Expelled”, and “K.A”.
[narrates] (p.215) “If we consider this to be the meaning, then at noon today, Ayanokouji will be expelled at I2…”
[trait] (p.214–216) She knows exactly what the note means and cannot ignore it; she burns a GPS Search and follows his signal F3→G3 to I2; her designated area I7 is sacrificed; she convinces herself it is a clue from someone else, not a trap — someone who cares.
[rel:ayanokouji-kiyotaka] (p.214–216) She drops her own exam goal to tail him to I2.

### y2v4 p.216–220 — Ch.6: Ibuki catches up; the score game
[scene] (p.216) On the border I4/I3 she spots a girl glaring at her from behind — Ibuki, panting; spent three GPS searches to find her; they fence over their scores for a while.
[says:ibuki-mio] (p.216) “Ah! Finally caught up! Wait, Horikita!”
[says] (p.216) “…Why are you here?”
[says:ibuki-mio] (p.217) “Score, show me the score.”
[says] (p.217) “Wait, what are you on about?”
[says:ibuki-mio] (p.217) “Didn’t I say? I’m not going to lose to you in this special exam.”
[says] (p.217) “There’s no need to confirm it now though? The exam isn’t over yet.”
[says:ibuki-mio] (p.217) “After the exam is over, nobody will know if the school will publish all the scores, right?”
[says] (p.218) “Maybe so. After all, it’s only the top and bottom ranking groups that really matter.”
[says:ibuki-mio] (p.218) “Huh? Then why don’t you look at it?”
[says] (p.218) “This is so stupid, I can’t believe it… You purposely came here for this, seriously? How many times did you use GPS search?”
[says:ibuki-mio] (p.218) “… 3 times. Because you were right next to me, I thought this would be the only time where I could do this.”
[says] (p.218) “Poor you.”
[says:ibuki-mio] (p.218) “I don’t need your sympathy, tell me your score, I’ve got 131 points!”
[says] (p.218) “Thanks for telling me so casually. But I want to say two things. First, I can’t guarantee that you’re telling me your real score.”
[says] (p.218) “Second, even if you disclose your real score, I won’t tell you my score.”
[says:ibuki-mio] (p.218) “Huh? What did you just say? Are you saying the same thing as that bastard?”
[says] (p.218) “Although we’re both 2nd years, we’re enemies. I don’t want to risk leaking information.”
[says:ibuki-mio] (p.219) “I know now. You’re scared after hearing my score? You’re losing, right?”
[says] (p.219) “As for whether I’m winning or losing, I’m not going to answer that either.”
[says:ibuki-mio] (p.219) “How about you just admit it? Admit that you can’t beat my score.”
[says] (p.219) “Fine then, just go back to your exam.”
[says:ibuki-mio] (p.219) “… You’re so irritating. Come on, just show me your score!”
[says] (p.219) “I’ve conceded defeat, aren’t you satisfied?”
[says:ibuki-mio] (p.219) “I want to know your real score. I want to know by how big of a margin I beat you.”
[says:ibuki-mio] (p.219) “How dull…”
[says:ibuki-mio] (p.219) “It’s important to me.”
[says] (p.219) “Sorry, I’m in a hurry.”
[says:ibuki-mio] (p.219) “Are you running away?”
[says] (p.219) “I’m rushing to get to my designated area, so it would be a bit strange to describe it as running away.”
[says] (p.220) “Do you have a designated area in the north? Or are you just chasing me?”
[says:ibuki-mio] (p.220) “What I want to know now is your score. If I know that, I will go back to my designated area too.”
[says] (p.220) “…I lost to you.”
[says:ibuki-mio] (p.220) “Ah, admit it? You finally admit to losing?”
[says] (p.220) “No, I mean I lost to your persistence. My score is 145 points. It’s a pity that even though you came here in person, I still win.”
[says:ibuki-mio] (p.220) “You won against me? If you won, give me proof, proof.”
[trait] (p.216–220) She shows her tablet to Ibuki — 145 points; the psychological price of confirming the gap: Ibuki screams; she waves her off and keeps walking.
[rel:ibuki-mio] (p.216–220) Her sworn rival; reveals her own score only to end the chase.

### y2v4 p.220–226 — Ch.6: Amasawa stops her
[scene] (p.220) The two girls part ways; Amasawa appears from nowhere with a childlike smile and stops Horikita; reveals the note is hers and she staked her own position to be here.
[says:amasawa-ichika] (p.220) “Found you.”
[says:amasawa-ichika] (p.221) “Hello, Horikita-senpai!”
[says] (p.221) “…Who are you?”
[says:amasawa-ichika] (p.221) “Class 1-A’s Amasawa Ichika.”
[says:amasawa-ichika] (p.221) “You guys don’t need to mind me, just continue doing your own thing, okay~~?”
[says] (p.221) “That’s not okay, we’re talking about something personal.”
[says:ibuki-mio] (p.221) “You’re a nuisance.”
[says:amasawa-ichika] (p.221) “Is Sudo-senpai okay? Horikita-senpai.”
[says:amasawa-ichika] (p.222) “….Well, thanks for saving him, I’m grateful.”
[says:ibuki-mio] (p.222) “Didn’t I say that you’re a nuisance? I have business with her, so get out of here.”
[says:amasawa-ichika] (p.222) “Business? Didn’t Ibuki-senpai run over here without permission?”
[says] (p.222) “Even if that’s the case, it’s still fine.”
[says] (p.222) “What’s your purpose, Amasawa-san?”
[says] (p.222) “Fine.”
[says:amasawa-ichika] (p.222) “I want to ask you a question. Where are you going, Horikita-senpai?”
[says] (p.222) “Right now, I’m standing and chatting with Ibuki-san, so when I finish with her, I’ll immediately rush to F3.”
[says:amasawa-ichika] (p.223) “It’s not good to lie, Horikita-senpai! Your designated area isn’t in this direction right?”
[says] (p.223) “What do you mean? Are you trying to make me fall into a trap using strange methods?”
[says:amasawa-ichika] (p.223) “Misleading is useless though. The designated area you were supposed to go to is I7, isn’t it?”
[says] (p.223) “Isn’t it a given to be wary of the people trying to frame Ayanokouji-kun?”
[says:amasawa-ichika] (p.223) “Oh, so that’s it. Perhaps.”
[says:amasawa-ichika] (p.224) “Where are you planning to go, Horikita-senpai? It’s not I2, is it?”
[says] (p.224) “You’ve seen through a lot, huh. But I decided to go to I2 because of what happened this morning. You’re really observant, aren’t you?”
[says] (p.224) “Ibuki-san.”
[says:ibuki-mio] (p.224) “Ah, seriously? Well that sucks.”
[says] (p.225) “If you’re satisfied now, go along to your designated area. On the last day, the score you’ll get is twice as high, so you still have a chance to bounce back.”
[says:ibuki-mio] (p.225) “Yeah, but… What do you mean by abandoning the designated area?”
[says] (p.225) “Ibuki, this is a great opportunity. I’m in a state where I can’t score right now.”
[says:ibuki-mio] (p.225) “Indeed, the winner and loser will be decided at the end of this uninhabited island exam. If you say you’re going to stop now, I won’t hesitate to make a comeback for good.”
[says] (p.226) “I’m going to I2 now, what are you going to do?”
[says:amasawa-ichika] (p.226) “Why are you going to such an irrelevant area as I2? There’s not even an event there. Is this something you should be doing during a special exam?”
[says] (p.226) “Don’t tell me you don’t know why?”
[says:amasawa-ichika] (p.226) “What do you mean?”
[says] (p.226) “Don’t play dumb, what’s the purpose in throwing this piece of paper in the tent when I was asleep?”
[says:amasawa-ichika] (p.226) “…Paper? Is that for me?”
[says:amasawa-ichika] (p.226) “It’s a string of irregularly arranged words… “Noon”, “K.A”, “Expelled”, “I2”.”
[says:amasawa-ichika] (p.226) “I really hate… how you like to play games, even to this extent…”
[says] (p.226) “Games? What are you planning to involve me and Ayanokouji-kun in?”
[says:amasawa-ichika] (p.226) “This, I don’t know. Because I’m just one of the bystanders, just like you.”
[says] (p.226) “Don’t misunderstand. You appearing in front of me is the best proof that you wrote this.”
[says] (p.226) “Looking at those 4 words, what kind of bad feeling are you experiencing?”
[says:amasawa-ichika] (p.226) “Ayanokouji may be expelled. It’s not hard to understand it like that.”
[says:amasawa-ichika] (p.227) “There’s something I got out of our conversation. Something’s gonna happen at I2. Otherwise, you wouldn’t try so hard to stop me here.”
[says:amasawa-ichika] (p.227) “About Ayanokouji-senpai’s situation. You don’t know anything about that, right, Horikita-senpai?”
[says] (p.227) “So, are you saying that you know him better than I do?”
[says:amasawa-ichika] (p.227) “That’s right. I know Ayanokouji-senpai very well. Why is he so handsome, so smart… and so much stronger than anyone else?”
[says:amasawa-ichika] (p.228) “Then, what do you know, Horikita-senpai?”
[says] (p.228) “Yeah, it’s true. I probably know nothing about him. I can’t deny that.”
[says] (p.228) “But?”
[says] (p.228) “from now until graduation, I want to continue understanding him better, as my classmate… As a friend, more than you are now.”
[says:amasawa-ichika] (p.229) “Do you think you can help? Horikita-senpai.”
[says] (p.229) “Maybe I’m not strong enough right now, but I intend to help him when he’s in trouble.”
[says:amasawa-ichika] (p.230) “Don’t kid me, how could Ayanokouji-senpai need your help?”
[says] (p.230) “That’s true, at least for now.”
[says] (p.230) “One more thing, I learned that you genuinely don’t want me to go to I2, which means you’re not the one who wrote that.”
[says:amasawa-ichika] (p.230) “I can’t let you go over there, Horikita-senpai.”
[says] (p.230) “The more you try to stop me, the more I must go to I2. From your tone of voice, shouldn’t he be having a hard time now?”
[says:amasawa-ichika] (p.230) “Do you think you get past me?”
[says] (p.230) “Yes, I do think I can get past you.”
[says:amasawa-ichika] (p.230) “Well~ Your determination has been conveyed to me. I’ll only wait until you put your backpack down.”
[says:amasawa-ichika] (p.231) “Let me tell you something first. I have experience with martial arts.”
[says] (p.231) “I already knew that.”
[says:amasawa-ichika] (p.231) “…Ah, you really are an information warrior.”
[says:amasawa-ichika] (p.231) “Let me also say right now that I’m super powerful, so you better keep that in mind.”
[trait] (p.220–231) She admits she knows almost nothing about him — Amasawa is the information warrior who knows him deeply; she sets I2 as her own destination; Amasawa physically blocks her; Horikita strips to a fighting stance.
[rel:amasawa-ichika] (p.220–231) The note writer; she challenges Horikita physically; she taunts her with Amasawa-level knowledge of Ayanokōji.
[rel:ibuki-mio] (p.224–226) Sends her to I7 to save her from the aftermath.

### y2v4 p.232–236 — Ch.6: Amasawa's demonstration
[scene] (p.232) Fight at I2's border; Amasawa fights with minimal movement; Horikita is smashed to the ground in one throw; a kick to her ribs; Amasawa pins her down like a game.
[says] (p.232) “Huh?!”
[says] (p.232) “Impossible…”

[says] (p.233) “I’ll do that, as long as your motive isn’t to stop me from going to Ayanokouji-kun.”
[says] (p.234) “Again.”
[says:amasawa-ichika] (p.234) “You thought I just used judo, didn’t you? That’s too naive of you.”
[says] (p.235) “The 1st years this year, they aren’t cute at all…”

[says] (p.18) "That's fine. I don't want to waste my time with a random idea."

[scene] (p.60) In his room, she delivers the real reason for visiting: she cannot determine who voted which way no matter how much they discuss — and she names Kushida and Kōenji as the likely dissenters.
